Um... I don't think so. I mean, I guess sometimes I might do things without planning them out completely, but... I haven't really noticed it being a big problem.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless or fidgety when you're not in a situation where you need to sit still?\n\nPatient: I suppose so. Um... even if I look like I'm just sitting there calmly, I usually feel... I don't know, this sort of pressure inside. Like I need to be moving, or switching what I'm doing. Or at least doing something with my hands... otherwise I feel like I'm going to crawl out of my skin. So, yeah. I guess I do feel restless a lot.\n\nDoctor: As a kid, did you often climb on things or run excessively?\n\nPatient: Um... not really? I don't think so. I mean, I played outside like anyone else, but... I don't remember constantly climbing on things or running around when I wasn't supposed to. So... no, probably not.\n\nDoctor: Do you often interrupt others when they're speaking?\n\nPatient: I don't believe so. Um... I usually just wait for them to finish. I mean, nobody has ever really complained to me about it, so... no, I don't think I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you often have trouble organizing your tasks or activities?\n\nPatient: I suppose. Um... things just kind of end up everywhere. Like my work tickets, or... notes for fixing things around the house, budget reminders. They just get scattered. Between Slack, sticky notes, my email, my phone... I don't know. I guess I spend more time trying to figure out what I'm supposed to do than actually getting it done. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Did you often have trouble following through on instructions or finishing your homework when you were in school?\n\nPatient: I don't really remember much about school, to be honest. But... I guess I do struggle with instructions sometimes now. \n\nUm... like, if my husband asks me to call the insurance company, upload a form, and then email the confirmation... I usually do the first part. But somehow the last step just... sits unfinished. Until he reminds me about it. So... yeah, I suppose so.\n\nDoctor: Do you often talk excessively?\n\nPatient: Not really. Um... I usually keep to myself, mostly. At work, I'm usually just looking at my screens and coding, so... no, I wouldn't say that.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced these feelings of restlessness and trouble staying seated for most of your life?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess so. I mean, I don't remember a lot, but... I do remember teachers in elementary school having to move my desk. Or, you know, constantly telling me to sit down. I was always popping out of my seat, or blurting things out before it was my turn. And I'd just rush through my work, even when I knew how to do it. So... yeah, it's been a while.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself becoming so focused on a task or activity that you lose track of time and forget about everything else?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I don't think I do that. I mean, I do my work, but... I usually know what time it is. So, no.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever become so absorbed in an activity that you forget about everything else around you?\n\nPatient: Um... I think you just asked me that, actually. But... no. I don't really get absorbed like that. I usually stay pretty aware of what's going on around me.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
